2026-11-05T23:35:00.6700228Z ##[start-action display=Set Trivy environment variables;id=__aquasecurity_trivy-action.__run_3]
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7163089Z ##[group]Run # Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7163839Z [36;1m# Note: There is currently no way to distinguish between undefined variables and empty strings in GitHub Actions.[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7164399Z [36;1m# This limitation affects how we handle default values and empty inputs.[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7164906Z [36;1m# For more information, see: https://github.com/actions/runner/issues/924[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7165271Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7165608Z [36;1m# The following logic implements the configuration priority described in the README:[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7165996Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7166202Z [36;1m# Inputs[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7166424Z [36;1m# Environment Variables[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7166676Z [36;1m# Config File[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7166899Z [36;1m# Defaults[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7167109Z [36;1m#[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7167486Z [36;1m# As noted above defaults are awkward to handle as GitHub Actions will inject those values as the input[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7168200Z [36;1m# if the caller doesn't provide them, thus if the input matches the default we don't set it as we[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7168797Z [36;1m# can't tell the difference.  Plus if we did set it when it was the default value then it could potentially[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7169611Z [36;1m# override an external environment variable, or something in the callers configuration file, which then wouldn't[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7170137Z [36;1m# match the configuration priority that is documented.[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7170356Z [36;1mset_env_var_if_provided() {[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7170531Z [36;1m  local var_name="$1"[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7170696Z [36;1m  local input_value="$2"[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7170866Z [36;1m  local default_value="$3"[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7171026Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7171209Z [36;1m  if [ -n "$input_value" ] && [ "$input_value" != "$default_value" ]; then[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7171490Z [36;1m    # If action was provided with explicit input by the caller set that[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7171795Z [36;1m    # Use printf %q to safely escape special characters and prevent command injection[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7172097Z [36;1m    printf 'export %s=%q\n' "$var_name" "$input_value" >> trivy_envs.txt[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7172310Z [36;1m  fi[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7172439Z [36;1m}[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7172566Z [36;1m[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7172752Z [36;1m# Set environment variables, handling those with default values[0m
2026-11-05T23:35:00.7173086Z [36;1m# cf. https://aquasecurity.github.io/trivy/latest/docs/configuration/#environment-variables[0m
